Conclusion: 所提出的无监督、自适应、拓扑驱动的生成式统计回放方法，是一种可扩展、灵活的持续学习新范式，有效解决了存储开销与性能平衡问题，具备广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: This work presents a generative continual learning framework based on growing self-organizing maps (GSOMs) that are augmented with learned distributional statistics as well as encoder-decoder models for class-incremental learning. The proposed approach enables exemplar-free replay using distributional statistical memory, which eliminates the need to store raw data. Each GSOM unit maintains its own mean, variance, and covariance estimates, which are subsequently used to generate synthetic samples for replay; in encoder-decoder configurations, these samples are then decoded back into the input space (via ancestral sampling) for subsequent training. Our method is fully unsupervised, as it does not rely on explicit task boundaries or class labels during training. Results across multiple benchmarks show that the proposed approach achieves performance competitive even with supervised state-of-the-art memory-based methods while consistently outperforming memory-free approaches. In several settings, our framework matches or exceeds existing baselines, particularly in challenging single-class incremental scenarios. We also provide baseline results for single-class incremental TinyImageNet and MiniImageNet, offering a useful reference for future work. This work highlights the effectiveness of an unsupervised, adaptive, topology-driven neural form of statistical replay as a scalable, flexible approach to continual learning.

</details>


### [238] [SegBench-GC: Testing Segmentation Invariance in Multi-Step Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27678)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出SegBench-GC，一个针对分割不变性的受控压力测试，用于评估离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中人工分割边界对性能的影响。通过固定轨迹、目标采样和优化设置，仅改变分割边界及其是否保留延续值，发现当分割边界被当作吸收态时，性能显著下降；而采用延续有效目标（CVT）则能保持性能稳定。实验表明，不当处理分割边界会导致多步离线GCRL严重失效，且这种问题在不同学习器中普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线目标条件强化学习方法依赖轨迹结构进行未来目标采样和多步目标计算，但实际日志轨迹可能因管理原因被人为分割，这些分割点并不对应真实终止状态。这可能导致多步目标估计错误，影响学习效果。因此需要一个受控基准来检验分割处理方式对算法性能的影响。

Method: 提出SegBench-GC基准，通过在真实轨迹上添加35,000个人工分割边界，对比三种分割处理方式：未切割（uncut）、延续有效目标（CVT）和将切割点视为吸收态（naive）。所有其他设置保持一致，包括目标采样、优化配置与评估方式。使用目标级诊断验证理论目标与数值结果的一致性，并通过学习的批评者分析偏差来源。

Result: 在PointMaze任务中，未切割情况下成功率为50.5%，使用CVT为39.1%，而将切割点视为吸收态时仅为19.1%；不同分割实现下，朴素平均成功率介于4.8%至31.9%之间。在Puzzle-4x5任务中，同样观察到类似失败模式，说明该问题具有普遍性。目标诊断显示理论与数值高度一致，而朴素处理导致学习批评者出现显著乐观偏差，CVT则保持与未切割情况相近。

Conclusion: 行政分割边界若未正确处理，会严重影响多步离线目标条件强化学习的性能。延续有效目标（CVT）通过标准延续引导机制而非引入新贝尔曼规则，可有效缓解此问题。本研究贡献在于构建了可控基准、隔离失败根源，并提供了跨学习器证据，证明分割处理方式是影响离线GCRL性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) often uses trajectory structure for future-goal sampling and multi-step targets, yet logged trajectories may be partitioned for administrative reasons that do not correspond to termination. We introduce SegBench-GC, a controlled stress test of segmentation invariance that holds transitions, source trajectories, goal sampling, optimization settings, and evaluation fixed while varying only artificial backup boundaries and whether those boundaries retain continuation value. Continuation-valid targets (CVT) provide the segmentation-consistent control: reward accumulation stops at an artificial cut, but the target bootstraps from its stored successor. In a matched-count PointMaze study with 35,000 artificial cuts, three segmentation realizations, and three optimization seeds, final 50-episode-per-task success is 50.5% uncut, 39.1% with CVT, and 19.1% when the same cuts are treated as absorbing; across segmentation realizations, naive mean success ranges from 4.8% to 31.9%. An independent published n-step baseline (n=25) from the Decoupled Q-Chunking codebase shows the same failure on Puzzle-4x5: 47.2% uncut, 58.5% CVT, and 0.27% naive across three optimization seeds. A target-level diagnostic verifies the analytic target difference to numerical precision, and learned-critic diagnostics show a large optimistic shift under naive handling while CVT remains approximately aligned with the uncut critic. CVT applies standard continuation bootstrapping rather than a new Bellman rule; the contribution is the controlled benchmark, failure isolation, and cross-learner evidence that administrative segmentation can materially change multi-step offline GCRL.

</details>


### [239] [SafeStep: An Interactive Demonstration of Semantic Communication for Pedestrian Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27688)
*Christian McDowell,Andrea Panebianco,Jeremiah Yang,Sirin Chakraborty,Samuel Chamoun,Travis Ross,Yin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeStep是一个基于浏览器的交互式语义通信平台，用于实时行人安全监控。它从四个实时交通摄像头获取行人信息，通过语义通信收发器在AWGN信道上传输，并在浏览器中渲染用户特定的位置、轨迹和风险标签。平台支持独立选择收发器、信噪比（SNR）、码长和信息年龄（AoI），并实时展示所选配置下的性能。与五种基线收发器相比，新提出的Meta-VIB设计在不进行在线重训练的情况下，仅用416万参数即可跨不同SNR、码长和AoI值实现良好泛化，实验表明其平均任务损失降低高达92.1%。在单个高端GPU服务器上，系统可支持20名用户并发访问，保持5帧/秒的目标帧率；在100名用户请求不同配置时，无请求失败，平均应用响应时间低于1秒，但每浏览器平均帧率降至约1帧/秒。SafeStep是首个使信息年龄引起的下游退化在实时监控应用中直接可视化的语义通信平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信系统缺乏实时性与可交互性，难以在动态变化的通信条件下直观展示信息年龄（AoI）对下游任务性能的影响。为解决这一问题，本文提出SafeStep，旨在构建一个实时、可交互的语义通信平台，实现对行人安全的动态监控，并揭示通信参数变化对系统性能的实际影响。

Method: SafeStep平台集成多路实时视频流处理、语义通信收发器（如Meta-VIB）、AWGN信道建模、动态配置管理与浏览器端渲染。系统采用轻量级神经网络模型Meta-VIB，具备低参数量与强泛化能力，可在不同信噪比、码长和信息年龄条件下稳定工作。平台支持用户自定义配置，并通过浏览器实时反馈监控结果，同时记录系统性能指标。

Result: Meta-VIB在多种配置下表现出色，平均任务损失降低达92.1%；系统在20名用户下可维持5帧/秒的帧率；在100名用户并发且配置各异的情况下，无请求失败，平均响应时间<1秒，但每浏览器帧率下降至约1帧/秒。该平台首次实现了信息年龄导致的下游性能退化在真实监控场景中的可视化。

Conclusion: SafeStep是首个实现高实时性、可交互性与多配置支持的语义通信平台，能够直观展示通信参数（如信噪比、码长、信息年龄）对下游任务性能的影响。其核心贡献在于将语义通信的理论性能转化为实际可用的实时监控能力，为未来智能交通系统的通信优化提供了重要实验基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop SafeStep, an interactive browser-based semantic communication platform for live pedestrian safety monitoring. SafeStep extracts pedestrian information from four live traffic-camera feeds, transmits it through a semantic communication transceiver over an Additive White Gaussian Noise (AWGN) channel, and renders user-specific positions, trajectories, and risk labels. The platform allows to independently select the transceiver, Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR), codelength, and Age of Information (AoI), and demonstrates the transceiver performance of the selected configuration through live pedestrian safety monitoring to each browser. SafeStep compares a recently proposed semantic communication design called Meta-VIB with five baseline transceivers. Meta-VIB uses a compact neural model with only $4.16$ million parameters to generalize across varying SNR, codelength, and AoI values without online retraining. Experimental results show that Meta-VIB achieves mean task-loss reductions of up to $92.1\%$. On one high-end GPU server, the integrated concurrent-access workload maintains the target $5$ frames/s through $20$ users. At $100$ users, each requesting a distinct configuration, SafeStep records no request failures and a mean application response time below $1$ s, but its mean per-browser frame rate falls to approximately $1$ frame/s. To our knowledge, SafeStep is the first real-time semantic communication platform to make AoI-induced downstream degradation directly observable in live monitoring applications.

</details>


### [240] [RiskBlend: A Multi-Signal Framework for Test Input Prioritization in Machine Learning Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27704)
*Madhusudan Srinivasan,Namith Nishal Raphae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiskBlend 是一种无需依赖特定分类器的测试输入优先级排序框架，通过结合历史故障模式、预测变化、决策边界变化和邻域变化四种互补的风险信号，利用验证学习的 APFD 平方权重进行融合。在 1,200 种实验配置下，RiskBlend 在所有 80 个数据集-分类器-更新场景组合中均取得最高平均 APFD，相比最强基线提升高达 0.32，证明跨版本行为信号对发现机器学习系统中的回归错误具有重要价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当机器学习模型重新训练时，之前正确分类的输入可能被新版本错误分类，产生难以检测的回归故障。由于验证预测需要人工标注或专家评审等高成本操作，因此需通过测试输入优先级排序，在有限验证预算下最大化发现回归故障的数量。现有方法主要依赖单模型置信度分数，未充分挖掘模型版本间预测、决策边界和局部邻域的变化信息。

Method: 提出 RiskBlend 框架，整合四种风险信号：历史故障模式、预测偏移、决策边界偏移、局部邻域变化；使用验证阶段学习的 APFD 平方权重对这些信号进行加权融合，实现分类器无关的输入优先级排序。

Result: 在四个数据集、五种分类器、四种回归更新场景及 15 个随机种子共 1,200 次实验中，RiskBlend 在全部 80 组数据集-分类器-场景组合中均达到最高平均 APFD，性能优于现有方法，最大提升达 0.32。基于置信度的方法仅在稀疏类别特征的线性分类器上表现良好，归因于特征空间几何特性。

Conclusion: 跨版本的行为信号（如预测、边界、邻域变化）为识别机器学习系统中的回归故障提供了重要且互补的信息，显著提升测试输入优先级排序的有效性。

Abstract: When machine learning classifiers are retrained, inputs correctly classified by the previous model version may be misclassified by the updated version, creating regression faults that are costly to detect because verifying predictions against ground truth may require human annotation, expert review, or expensive simulation rather than inexpensive model inference. Test input prioritization addresses this problem by ranking inputs so that a limited verification budget reveals as many regression faults as possible. Existing approaches rely predominantly on single-model confidence scores and do not exploit how predictions, decision boundaries, and local neighborhoods change between model versions. We propose RiskBlend, a classifier-agnostic prioritization framework that combines four complementary risk signals: historical failure patterns, prediction shift, decision-boundary shift, and neighborhood change. These signals are combined using validation-learned APFD-squared weighting. Across four datasets, five classifiers, four regression-update scenarios, and 15 random seeds, totaling 1,200 experimental configurations, RiskBlend achieves the highest average APFD in all 80 dataset-classifier-scenario combinations, with improvements of up to 0.32 APFD over the strongest baseline. Confidence-based methods remain competitive primarily for linear classifiers on sparse categorical features, which we attribute to feature-space geometry. The results show that cross-version behavioral signals provide important complementary information for prioritizing regression faults in machine learning systems.

</details>


### [241] [DART-FL: Burst-Aware Multitask Federated Learning under Dynamic Inference Demand at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27713)
*Yiming Xie,Pinrui Yu,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DART-FL 是一种面向边缘智能系统的联合推理-训练资源调度框架，通过动态调整推理与训练的资源分配，并根据任务需求动态分配训练优先级，以提升高频任务在请求高峰期间的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上，推理和训练需共存，且推理需求随时间波动，因此需要平衡推理服务等级目标（SLO）与训练效率，确保高需求任务能及时优化。

Method: DART-FL 采用基于排队感知的 DPP 优化调度器，结合推理积压和系统服务能力确定最小推理资源需求，剩余资源按任务需求动态分配，并映射为动态损失权重，使高需求任务在早期通信轮次中获得更强训练强调。模型采用共享主干+任务特定头部结构，通过 FedAvg 聚合多任务模型。

Result: 在 Stanford Cars 和 Oxford Flowers 102 数据集上，使用合成及阿里真实工作负载测试表明，DART-FL 能有效适应时变推理需求，将高需求任务的学习进度前移至请求高峰期，显著提升高频任务的准确率，同时保持长期多任务性能稳定。

Conclusion: DART-FL 成功实现了推理-训练资源的协同自适应，提升了边缘多任务学习系统对动态工作负载的响应能力，在保障 SLO 的前提下优化了关键任务的训练效率。

Abstract: Edge intelligence systems increasingly require model training and online inference to coexist on resource-constrained devices, while inference demand can vary substantially across tasks over time. This creates two coupled challenges: sufficient computation must be reserved for inference to maintain service-level objectives (SLOs), while the remaining training capacity should adapt to task-specific demand so that frequently requested tasks can improve earlier during training.
  We propose an SLO-aware, demand-driven multitask federated learning framework (DART-FL) that jointly adapts the inference-training resource split and task-level training emphasis. At each scheduling interval, DART-FL uses the inference backlog and profiled service capacity to determine the minimum resource allocation required for inference. The remaining training capacity is then distributed across tasks using a queue-aware DPP-inspired scheduler, and the resulting task allocations are mapped to dynamic loss weights. This allows tasks experiencing higher inference demand to receive greater training emphasis in earlier communication rounds. Clients train a shared backbone with task-specific heads, and the complete multitask model is aggregated through FedAvg.
  We evaluate DART-FL using Stanford Cars and Oxford Flowers 102 under both synthetic and real Alibaba trace-derived workloads. Results show that DART-FL dynamically adapts the inference-training resource split to time-varying inference demand and shifts the learning progress of high-demand tasks toward their burst periods, improving model accuracy when those tasks are frequently requested while maintaining comparable long-term multitask performance.

</details>


### [242] [Beyond Non-IID: Learner--Client Distribution Mismatch in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27715)
*Yiming Xie,Lili Su,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习中学习者与客户端群体之间的分布不匹配问题，提出一种基于代理数据的动态、影响感知的客户端选择框架。通过在学习者特定的代理集上使用留一法评估，估计每个客户端对优化目标的潜在贡献，从而优先选择最有信息量的来源，有效缓解统计噪声和数据异质性的影响。实验表明，该方法在CIFAR-10上的异构数据划分下显著优于静态和动态基线，实现更快收敛和更高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习实践通常假设客户端聚合数据分布能代表学习者的目标准确分布，但现实中这一假设常不成立。传统客户端选择策略忽略这种分布错配，而现有多源迁移学习方法或需直接访问本地数据，或采用一次性模型/特征聚合，难以应对动态异质环境。因此亟需一种能识别有益客户端并适应变化环境的新方法。

Method: 提出一种动态、影响感知的客户端选择框架，利用学习者持有的小规模代理数据集，通过留一法评估各客户端的代理影响信号，以估计其对学习者优化目标的潜在贡献，并据此动态筛选最有益的知识源。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，面对不同异构数据划分，所提方法在多个指标上均优于静态与动态基线方法，表现出更快速的收敛速度和更高的模型精度。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了联邦学习中学习者与客户端群体间分布不匹配的挑战，提出了一种基于代理影响信号的动态客户端选择机制，能够有效识别高价值客户端，提升模型性能与训练效率，为实际部署中的异构联邦学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Federated learning systems are increasingly deployed to facilitate collaborative model training across a heterogeneous client population. Existing practice mostly implicitly assumes that the aggregated client data distribution is representative of the learner's target distribution or that learning from all available clients is uniformly beneficial for the learner distribution. However, such an assumption often does not hold in reality. Traditional client selection strategies in FL literature largely overlook such misalignment, while most existing work on multi-source transfer learning either requires direct access to local data or uses one-shot model/feature aggregation.
  In this paper, we take the initiative to understand and mitigate the impacts of such learner-client population misalignment. In particular, we consider the practical setting where the learner keeps a small proxy dataset. We observe that client contributions vary significantly across training rounds, and traditional technology is insufficient to identify beneficial sources under multi-source transfer diversity. Then, we propose a dynamic, influence-aware client selection framework that estimates each client's potential utility to the learner's optimization objective using proxy influence signals on a learner-specific proxy set. Via using leave-one-out evaluations, we prioritize the most informative sources of knowledge while controlling the negative impacts of statistical noise and data heterogeneity. Experiments on CIFAR-10 under heterogeneous data partitions demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms static and dynamic baselines, achieving faster convergence and higher accuracy.

</details>


### [243] [Leveraging a Foundation Model for the EEG-Based Diagnosis of Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27719)
*Maggie Lin,Chung-Lin Hou,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大型脑模型（LaBraM）的阿尔茨海默病（AD）诊断框架，利用预训练于2500小时以上EEG数据的模型提取高维潜在表示，并结合非线性随机森林分类器，在仅8秒EEG片段下实现高效精准诊断。在独立受试者5折交叉验证中，该方法达到ROC-AUC 89.36% ± 3.49%、PR AUC 81.45% ± 4.43%、平衡准确率82.44% ± 4.34%，显著优于传统频谱基线方法。后处理遮蔽分析证实模型捕捉到临床已知生物标志物（如枕叶-额叶α/θ节律退化），且预测概率与认知功能下降、临床严重程度及异常功率特征显著相关。结果表明，深度潜在表征可从噪声信号中提取临床相关的神经生理签名，实现快速、精确、数据高效的疾病识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病存在显著的生物学异质性，传统线性方法难以捕捉其复杂的非线性神经动态，导致诊断困难。亟需一种能有效处理高维、非线性脑电数据并提取可靠生物标志物的新方法。

Method: 采用预训练于超过2500小时EEG数据的大型脑模型（LaBraM）生成高维潜在嵌入，结合非线性随机森林分类器进行疾病分类。使用8秒短时段EEG输入，通过主题无关的5折交叉验证评估性能，并辅以遮蔽分析和神经生理对齐分析验证模型可解释性与临床相关性。

Result: 在区分痴呆患者与健康对照的测试中，模型在5折交叉验证下获得ROC-AUC 89.36% ± 3.49%、PR AUC 81.45% ± 4.43%、平衡准确率82.44% ± 4.34%。模型成功识别出临床已知的生物标志物（如α/θ节律退化），且预测概率与认知衰退、临床严重度及异常脑电参数显著相关。

Conclusion: 深度学习生成的潜在表征能够从噪声丰富的脑电数据中提取具有临床意义的神经生理签名，支持快速、精确且数据高效的阿尔茨海默病诊断，为未来个性化神经疾病筛查提供了新范式。

Abstract: Biological heterogeneity in Alzheimer's Disease (AD) poses a critical diagnostic challenge, particularly for traditional linear methods that fail to capture non-linear neural dynamics. To address this, we propose a diagnostic framework utilizing the Large Brain Model (LaBraM), pretrained on over 2,500 hours of EEG data. By integrating these high-dimensional latent embeddings with a non-linear Random Forest classifier, our approach effectively isolates robust disease markers. Under a rigorous subject-independent 5-fold cross-validation protocol, the method achieves an ROC-AUC of 89.36% +/- 3.49%, PR AUC of 81.45% +/- 4.43%, and Balanced Accuracy of 82.44% +/- 4.34% in distinguishing dementia patients from healthy controls. Notably, this performance uses only 8-second EEG segments, surpassing traditional spectral baselines, including band-power and parameterized oscillatory features (FOOOF). Post-hoc occlusion analysis confirms the model captures clinically validated biomarkers, specifically occipital-frontal Alpha and Theta rhythm degradation. Additional neurophysiological alignment analysis demonstrated that higher LaBraM-predicted dementia probability significantly correlated with worse cognitive performance, greater clinical severity, increased theta and alpha relative power, and higher aperiodic exponent. These findings demonstrate that deep latent representations extract clinically relevant signatures from noisy signals, enabling precise, rapid, and data-efficient diagnosis.

</details>


### [244] [Diffusion Distillation for Efficient Weather Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27728)
*Yiming Yang,Valentin Brekke,James Briant,Serge Guillas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种监督能量距离蒸馏方法，将多步扩散模型压缩为单步学生模型，通过对齐学生预测与教师样本及真实观测来提升天气预报精度。实验表明，该方法在全局预报和台风路径预测中表现优异，能有效保留极端事件的预报技能，且每自回归步仅需一次神经网络评估，性能优于现有蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能生成高质量的天气集合，但其迭代采样过程计算成本高，亟需高效替代方案。

Method: 采用监督能量距离蒸馏，将多步扩散教师模型压缩为单步学生模型，通过匹配学生输出与教师样本及真实观测来优化预测性能。

Result: 在全局天气预报和台风路径预测任务中，学生模型性能超越现有蒸馏方法，且在关键指标上达到或超过教师模型水平，同时显著降低计算开销。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功实现了高效、高精度的天气预报模型压缩，为实际应用提供了可行的轻量化解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate skillful weather ensembles but require costly iterative sampling. We introduce a supervised energy-distance distillation method that compresses a multi-step diffusion teacher into a single-step student by aligning student forecasts with teacher samples and ground-truth observations. Experiments on global forecasting and typhoon-track prediction show that our student outperforms existing distillation methods and preserves skill for extreme events. It matches or surpasses the teacher across key metrics using only one neural function evaluation per autoregressive step.

</details>


### [245] [The Calls are Coming from Inside the Model: Investigating Probe-based Detection of Tool-Calling Errors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27750)
*Eric Yeats,Brendan Kennedy,Loc Truong,John Buckheit,Jung Lee,Jesse Friedbaum,John Emanuello,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了使用线性探测器检测大型语言模型（LLM）中错误工具调用的有效性，发现探测方法能有效识别多种工具调用错误，包括参数值错误但类型正确的情况，且在不同模型规模、探测层和后训练类型下表现各异。探测器还具备对新型错误的泛化能力，适用于实际部署场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM系统越来越多地与外部世界交互，如何检测不当或错误的工具调用成为关注重点，传统日志框架难以捕捉某些错误，因此需要更有效的检测手段。

Method: 通过在线性探测器上对18个在伯克利工具调用排行榜上评估的LLM进行测试，分析其在不同模型规模、探测层和后训练类型下的探测效果，并考察其对新类型错误的泛化能力。

Result: 线性探测在检测多种工具调用错误方面表现出色，尤其能发现传统日志未记录的错误；模型规模、探测层和后训练类型是影响探测效果的关键因素；探测器具有良好的泛化能力，可适应新型错误。

Conclusion: 线性探测是一种有效且可推广的工具调用错误检测方法，适用于真实世界中复杂多变的LLM应用场景。

Abstract: The hidden states of large language models (LLMs) are known to capture rich information relating to model knowledge and behavior that can be hard to extract from examination of input and output alone. As LLM-based systems increasingly interface with the external world, one area of concern is detecting incorrect or improper use of tools. Motivated by this, we study the effectiveness of using linear probes to detect incorrect tool-calls, measuring probe efficacy across 18 tool-calling LLMs evaluated on the Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard. Overall, we find that probing is an effective means to catch a range of different tool-calling errors, including errors arising from using an argument that has the wrong value but the correct type, which might not be recorded by standard logging frameworks. Important factors in success include model size, probing layer, and model post-training type. We also show that probes are capable of generalizing to novel types of errors, which is critical in real world deployments.

</details>


### [246] [Beyond Search-Imitation: Prior-Directed Exploration for Searchless Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27757)
*Szymon Miłosz,Piotr Duch,Szymon Grabowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过自洽强化学习微调无搜索棋类网络，以提升单次前向传播下的棋力。采用基于网络自身MCTS先验的前向KL散度作为探索机制（优先探索先验认为有潜力的走法），并引入由价值头预测不确定性动态调节的采样温度，使决策在局面明朗后更精准。在约2000步训练后，谜题解答准确率从93.9%提升至94.9%，四步将杀准确率从77%升至81%，同时保持或略高于基线的无搜索棋力。实验表明，战术准确率与对弈强度可分离：准确率提升有限（+1个百分点），而棋力评级基本持平；仅在谜题上微调的对照组虽战术提升最大，但损失约260 Elo，说明更强的解题能力不等于更强的实战水平。分布分析揭示正则化的重要性：无正则化时自对弈会塌陷到单一变着路径，而新解决的难题多为先验曾保留但未被充分探索的“临界胜招”。前向KL先验在等级分上表现最优，与反向KL锚定相比，后者虽更集中但丢失了最难解的变着。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无搜索棋类网络依赖模仿强教师（如Leela Chess Zero）的搜索结果进行知识蒸馏，但模仿搜索过程并非有效模拟无搜索下的真实博弈策略。因此，需要一种更贴近实际无搜索对局的训练方式，以真正提升单次前向传播下的棋力与战术精度。

Method: 提出一种基于自对弈强化学习的微调方法，使用前向KL散度引导探索（即探索方向朝向网络自身MCTS先验所认为有潜力的走法），结合由价值头输出不确定性动态调整的采样温度，实现‘智能探索’与‘后期锐化’。通过少量训练步骤，在不牺牲搜索无依赖性能的前提下，显著提升战术准确性。

Result: 在10万谜题数据集上，谜题准确率从93.9%提升至94.9%，四步将杀准确率从77%提升至81%；棋力评级基本维持或略优于基线。对比实验显示，仅优化谜题准确率会导致棋力下降约260 Elo，证明战术准确率与整体棋力可分离。前向KL探索机制在等级分上表现最佳，优于反向KL锚定，且能保留更难解的变着支持。

Conclusion: 无搜索棋类网络可通过前向KL引导的探索与自适应温度机制，在极短训练时间内显著提升战术精度，同时保持甚至超越基线棋力。战术能力与对弈强度并非正相关，强调需在训练中平衡二者。前向KL探索优于传统反向KL，是提升鲁棒性和多样性的重要手段。

Abstract: Searchless chess networks reach human master strength from a single forward pass by imitating a stronger teacher: the strongest, Leela Chess Zero's (Lc0) released Chessformer, distills the visit counts of an AlphaZero-style Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). Imitating a search is a poor proxy for playing without one, so we fine-tune for single-pass strength with self-play reinforcement learning (RL). Its exploration is usually supplied by an entropy bonus, the reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence to uniform. We replace it with a forward, mass-covering KL toward the network's own MCTS prior (prior-directed exploration), so exploration covers the moves the prior judges promising, and pair it with an entropy-adaptive sampling temperature, set by the value head's outcome uncertainty, that sharpens once a position is decided. In about two thousand steps it raises puzzle accuracy from 93.9% to 94.9% on a 100,000-puzzle suite and mate-in-four accuracy from 77% to 81% while holding searchless strength at or slightly above the base. Measuring tactical accuracy and playing strength together across a matched-compute sweep, we find the two dissociate: accuracy gains fall in a one-point band while ratings straddle the base, and a control fine-tuned on puzzles alone posts the study's largest tactical gains while shedding roughly 260 Elo; a better puzzle-solver is not thereby a stronger player. Distribution-level measurements show what anchoring buys: without a regularizer self-play collapses onto a single line of play, and the puzzles newly solved are the near misses whose winning move the prior kept alive. The forward-KL prior tops the rating ladder, statistically tied with a reverse-KL anchor that concentrates twice as hard and drops the hardest solutions the mass-covering prior keeps in support.

</details>


### [247] [Fast Weight Attention for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27763)
*Yifan Zhang,Steve Ta,Jasper Zhang,Jichen Feng,Shuzhen Li,Yongxin Zhang,Yifeng Liu,Huizhuo Yuan,Mengdi Wang,Quanquan Gu,Andrew Chi-Chih Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于快速权重记忆和选择性状态空间模型的在线学习规则，通过将扩展上下文压缩为固定大小的递归状态，提出了一种在前缀预测目标下的局部快速记忆更新机制。针对平方误差回归与负内积目标，推导出归一化的一阶更新规则，提出了Falcon系列算法（如Falcon-1、Falcon-2、Falcon-3及其内积变体），并设计了递归、掩码并行和分块并行形式，结合数值稳定的正衰减重归一化。实验表明，该框架在语言建模中表现良好，并显著提升变量位数加法任务中的长度外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归序列模型在处理长序列时面临状态膨胀与记忆效率问题，亟需一种能够在线学习、高效压缩上下文且保持因果性的机制。本文旨在通过快速权重记忆与选择性状态空间模型，实现对时间对齐、可塑性、遗忘和有限回放的解耦，从而提升模型在长序列建模中的性能与泛化能力。

Method: 提出基于读写后自回归语义的局部快速记忆机制，定义前缀对齐的输入对 $(\mathbf{x}_t, \mathbf{y}_t) = (\varphi(\mathbf{k}_{t-1}), \mathbf{v}_t)$，构建归一化的一阶梯度更新规则，适用于平方误差与负内积目标。设计了Falcon-1至Falcon-3系列算法及其内积变体，提供递归、掩码并行与分块并行三种计算形式，并引入正衰减重归一化以保证数值稳定性。

Result: 所提方法在语言建模任务中保持竞争力，尤其在变量位数加法任务中显著提升长度外推能力；验证了该框架在分离时间对齐、可塑性、遗忘与回放机制方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了递归序列模型中时间对齐、可塑性、遗忘与有限回放的解耦，通过快速权重记忆与归一化更新机制，在保持因果性的同时提升了长序列建模能力，尤其在长度外推任务上表现优异。

Abstract: Recurrent fast-weight memories and selective state-space models compress an expanding context into a fixed-size recurrent state, making the state transition an online learning rule. We study this rule under read-after-write autoregressive semantics. For the prefix-prediction objective considered here, the local fast-memory example revealed at step $t$ is the prefix-aligned pair $(\mathbf{x}_t,\mathbf{y}_t)=(φ(\mathbf{k}_{t-1}),\mathbf{v}_t)$. The common same-step association $(φ(\mathbf{k}_t),\mathbf{v}_t)$ remains causal, but optimizes a different internal objective. We derive normalized first-order updates for squared-error regression and negative inner-product objectives. The regression family comprises Falcon-1 (a scalar NLMS update), Falcon-2 (its per-column extension), and Falcon-3 (a sliding-window mini-batch update); Falcon-1A/Falcon-2A/Falcon-3A are the corresponding inner-product variants. We provide recurrent, masked-parallel, and chunk-parallel forms, together with numerically stable positive-decay renormalization. Representative variants remain competitive in language modeling and improve length extrapolation on variable-digit addition. This framework separates temporal alignment, plasticity, forgetting, and bounded rehearsal in recurrent sequence models.

</details>


### [248] [Initialization Is Critical: Advancing Federated Short-Term Load Forecasting under Load Heterogeneity via Model Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27791)
*Jianing Chen,Vajiheh Farhadi,Yan Li,Thomas La Porta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在短期负荷预测中的应用，针对客户端数据存在结构异质性的问题，提出从全局和局部视角的两种模型初始化策略：利用公开辅助数据进行全局预训练初始化以减少客户端漂移；提出SLIAvg方法实现每轮通信中客户端逐步适应的本地初始化，提升训练一致性。所提方法兼容主流联邦学习框架与隐私保护技术，实验表明其可有效降低客户端漂移、改善收敛性并减少预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习在短期负荷预测中面临客户端数据结构异质性问题，即不同用户对外部因素响应不同、负荷时间模式各异，导致预测性能下降。同时，隐私保护需求推动使用联邦学习，但缺乏有效的初始化机制来应对异质性挑战。

Method: 提出两种初始化策略：1）全局预训练初始化，利用公开负载数据预先训练全局模型以减少客户端漂移；2）局部序列初始化（SLIAvg），在每轮通信中让客户端从逐步适应的模型开始训练，增强训练一致性。两种策略均仅修改初始化过程，兼容现有联邦学习框架与隐私保护技术。

Result: 在真实智能电表数据上基于两种典型预测架构的实验表明，所提策略显著降低了客户端漂移，改善了收敛行为，并有效减少了预测误差，验证了其有效性与普适性。

Conclusion: 通过引入全局预训练与局部序列初始化策略，能够有效缓解联邦短期负荷预测中的结构异质性问题，提升模型性能且不破坏隐私保护机制，具有良好的兼容性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Short-term load forecasting (STLF) provides essential information for numerous applications in modern power systems. However, accurate STLF often relies on fine-grained smart-meter data from distributed users, raising increasing concerns about data privacy. Federated learning (FL) has therefore emerged as a promising privacy-preserving paradigm for STLF. Nevertheless, this paper reveals structured heterogeneity in clients' load data. Specifically, clients exhibit different responses to exogenous factors and distinct temporal load profiles, which can degrade forecasting performance in FL. To mitigate these issues, this paper studies the role of model initialization in federated STLF, and proposes two initialization strategies from global and local perspectives. For global model initialization, when auxiliary public load data are available, a pretrained initialization strategy is developed to initialize the global model before federated training, thereby reducing client drift during the training process. For local model initialization, we propose SLIAvg, a sequential local initialization strategy that promotes a more consistent training process by allowing participating clients to start from progressively adapted models within each communication round. Since the proposed strategies only modify the initialization process, they are compatible with most existing FL frameworks and privacy-enhancing techniques. Experiments on real smart-meter data with two representative forecasting architectures demonstrate that the proposed strategies effectively improve forecasting performance, as evidenced by reduced client drift, improved convergence behavior, and lower forecasting errors.

</details>


### [249] [Node-wise Feature Encoding for Neural Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27794)
*Matthew Grenier,William Hammer,Andrew Heuer,Nikhil Krishna,Yi Wang,Ramtin Zand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FeatureFormer，一种结合节点级计算成本编码的神经性能预测模型，用于在资源受限设备上高效进行神经架构搜索。引入NNEQ数据集以统一评估延迟和能耗，并在多种场景下展示其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN和Transformer的预测模型忽略了节点级别的计算成本，限制了对关键性能操作的建模能力。

Method: 在门控图注意力架构中显式引入FLOPs、参数量和内存代理的节点级编码，构建FeatureFormer模型。

Result: FeatureFormer在延迟和能耗预测方面均达到当前最佳表现，包括具有挑战性的跨域设置；所提编码方法可广泛应用于现有预测器并带来一致提升，且开销极小。

Conclusion: FeatureFormer通过显式编码节点级计算成本，在性能预测任务中表现出色，为边缘设备上的神经架构搜索提供了高效工具。

Abstract: As neural networks are increasingly deployed on resource constrained edge devices, accurate prediction of latency and energy is critical for efficient neural architecture search. Existing GNN and transformer based predictors achieve strong results but largely ignore node-level computational cost, limiting their ability to model performance critical operations. To address this, we introduce FeatureFormer, a neural performance predictor that incorporates explicit node-wise encodings of FLOPs, parameter counts, and memory proxies within a gated graph attention architecture. We also present NNEQ, a new large-scale energy consumption dataset that enables unified evaluation of latency and energy prediction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FeatureFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance across both metrics, including challenging out-of-domain settings. Finally, we show that the proposed encoding is broadly applicable and consistently improves existing predictors with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [250] [Actionable CBFI: Integrating Structural Decomposition and Causal Counterfactual Recourse for Tabular Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27821)
*Sejong Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出A-CBFI框架，通过结构因果模型识别特征协同瓶颈与抑制性结构锁，实现高精度、低干预负担的可解释性决策支持，在金融与医疗领域验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法在因果有效性、认知负荷和预测性能方面存在不足，尤其在复杂非线性模型中难以提供精准且可操作的反事实建议。

Method: 基于结构因果模型（SCM），提出行动导向的案例化特征重要性（A-CBFI）框架，通过分离主动干预空间与下游影响，聚焦诊断出的根本原因进行靶向干预。

Result: 在金融与医疗领域实验中，A-CBFI将主动人类干预负担降低76.9%，同时保持与全面因果基线相当的全局救济成本，并实现所有因果可行实例的完全相对收敛。

Conclusion: A-CBFI实现了因果有效性与可操作性的统一，为复杂机器学习模型提供了高效、精准、可解释的反事实救济方案。

Abstract: Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) increasingly calls for actionable counterfactual recourse, yet current methodologies face challenges related to causal invalidity, excessive cognitive burden, and predictive failure. Exhaustive causal search algorithms often require modifications to multiple attributes, whereas additive attribution-guided methods, such as SHAP, ignore higher-order feature synergies, leading to suboptimal predictive momentum and diffuse intervention effort in complex nonlinear models, such as XGBoost. To bridge this gap, we introduce actionable case-based feature importance (A-CBFI), a diagnosis-prescription integrated framework for tabular machine learning. Grounded in structural causal models (SCMs), A-CBFI isolates synergistic interaction bottlenecks and releases suppressive structural locks, translating them into targeted interventions. By mathematically separating the active user intervention space (L_{\mathrm{active}}) from downstream effects and concentrating over 98.3% of the intervention effort on diagnosed root causes, A-CBFI enables highly targeted interventions. Empirical evaluations across the financial and healthcare domains demonstrate that A-CBFI reduces the active human intervention burden by 76.9% while maintaining comparable global recourse cost to exhaustive causal baselines. By prioritizing the diagnosed causal bottlenecks, A-CBFI provides targeted and actionable recourse while maintaining causal validity and achieving full relative convergence across all causally feasible instances.

</details>


### [251] [FedEHR-Agents: Federated Agentic Optimization for Automated EHR Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27856)
*Jun Bai,Ruilin Wang,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedEHR-Agents，一种以经验为中心的联邦代理优化框架，用于自动化电子健康记录（EHR）建模。该框架通过在各医院部署自主临床EHR代理，结合历史记忆、任务评估和TextGrad提示优化来提升本地建模经验，并由联邦服务器进行证据引导的经验聚合，将整合后的经验提炼为全局元提示以供本地进一步优化。在真实多中心EHR数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多种临床预测任务中优于本地及传统联邦基线方法，且对不同联邦规模和大语言模型（LLM）架构均表现稳健，证明了临床建模经验作为超越参数中心化联邦学习的合作对象的潜力，推动了联邦自主临床智能的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法主要聚焦于模型参数或其更新，忽略了自主代理在临床建模过程中积累的丰富经验；而医院间直接共享患者级EHR数据受限于隐私保护要求，因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能有效共享建模经验的新范式。

Method: FedEHR-Agents采用经验驱动的联邦学习框架：各医院部署自主临床代理，执行数据预处理与模型开发，并通过历史记忆、任务评估与TextGrad-based提示优化持续改进本地经验；联邦服务器则基于证据引导策略聚合来自异构医院的可靠且互补的建模经验，并将其提炼为全局元提示，供各机构本地迭代优化。

Result: 在多个真实世界多中心EHR基准测试中，FedEHR-Agents在各类临床预测任务上均显著优于本地训练与传统联邦学习基线方法，具备良好的鲁棒性，适用于不同联邦规模和不同的大语言模型基础架构。

Conclusion: 本研究首次将临床建模经验作为联邦协作的核心对象，突破了传统以参数为中心的联邦学习局限，为构建可扩展、隐私保护的自主临床智能系统提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models are enabling autonomous clinical agents to perform increasingly complex electronic health record (EHR) modeling workflows. However, agents deployed at individual hospitals remain constrained by institution-specific data and modeling environments, while direct cross-hospital collaboration is restricted by the sensitivity of patient-level EHR data. Although federated learning (FL) provides a natural foundation for privacy-preserving collaboration, existing approaches remain predominantly model-centric, limiting federation to prediction models or their updates while overlooking the richer modeling experience accumulated by autonomous agents. To address this limitation, we propose FedEHR-Agents, an experience-centric federated agentic optimization framework for automated EHR modeling. Each hospital deploys an autonomous clinical EHR agent that performs data preprocessing and model development while refining local clinical modeling experience through historical memory, task-specific evaluation, and TextGrad-based prompt refinement. The federated server performs evidence-guided experience aggregation to integrate reliable and complementary modeling experience across heterogeneous hospitals and distills the aggregated experience into global meta-prompts for subsequent local refinement. Extensive experiments on real-world multi-hospital EHR benchmarks demonstrate that FedEHR-Agents consistently outperforms local and federated baselines across diverse clinical prediction tasks and remains robust across different federation scales and LLM backbones. These results establish clinical modeling experience as a promising collaborative object beyond conventional parameter-centric FL and point toward federated autonomous clinical intelligence.

</details>


### [252] [SOMTab: Set-Order Mamba for Efficient Tabular In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27882)
*Hao Wang,Siyu Zhang,Wei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOMTab提出了一种基于Mamba的高效表格上下文学习架构，通过分离表示构建与查询条件检索，实现快速推理和低内存占用，在多个基准上接近Transformer模型性能，展现了优越的效率-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究注意力机制在表格上下文学习中是否必要于每个阶段，旨在提升模型效率同时保持高性能。

Method: SOMTab将表格标记映射为稳定潜在槽，并使用Mamba状态空间混合构建紧凑表示；最终预测阶段保留注意力机制以实现查询条件检索。引入DCH-TailMix合成先验以增强依赖结构多样性。

Result: SOMTab在多个表格基准上表现接近强Transformer模型，具备更快推理速度和更低GPU内存消耗，实现更优的效率-精度平衡。

Conclusion: 注意力并非表格上下文学习中每个阶段的必需组件；通过合理设计架构，可实现高效且高性能的表格式学习。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models based on in-context learning have recently emerged as strong alternatives to task-specific model fitting. However, the current performance frontier remains dominated by attention-heavy architectures, where attention is used throughout the modeling pipeline. This raises a natural question: is attention necessary at every stage of tabular in-context learning? We introduce SOMTab, a Set-Order Mamba architecture for efficient tabular in-context learning. SOMTab separates representation construction from query-conditioned retrieval. For row and column representations, it maps unordered table tokens into stable latent slots and applies Mamba-based state-space mixing to construct compact representations. For final prediction, it retains attention-based in-context learning to preserve query-conditioned retrieval from labeled context examples. We further introduce DCH-TailMix, a synthetic prior that combines degree-corrected graph heterogeneity with mixed heavy-tailed regimes to diversify synthetic dependency structures. Across tabular benchmarks, SOMTab approaches the performance of strong Transformer-based tabular foundation models while achieving faster inference and lower GPU memory usage, yielding a favorable efficiency--accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [253] [Beyond Pairwise Graphs in Science: Hypergraph Adaptive Wavelet Operators for Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27883)
*Rajat Sarkar,Venkataramana Runkana,Souvik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HALO 是一种新型神经算子，通过将物理系统建模为超图并学习其谱小波域中的映射，解决了复杂几何下传统网格方法精度损失的问题。它采用切比雪夫多项式小波滤波器避免了显式的超图拉普拉斯特征分解，在保持线性稀疏矩阵计算成本的同时实现局部化频域核。其可训练的二元小波尺度被正则化以覆盖紧框架，使频率响应能自适应不同偏微分方程（PDE），并确保多尺度频谱稳定性。在二维和三维结构/非结构化网格基准测试中，HALO 在频率、Transformer、DeepONet、状态空间及图基基线中表现最佳或接近最佳，并支持稳定多步前推。模型还成功扩展至工业级气动几何，即使在数十万点的网格上也达到或超越最强固定离散化Transformer的表现，且保持分辨率等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子在处理复杂几何时受限于规则网格，导致精度下降；而图神经网络虽能处理非结构化网格，但难以捕捉单元间群组耦合与局部守恒体积关系。因此需要一种既能处理非结构化域又具备多尺度频域建模能力的新方法。

Method: 提出超图自适应小波算子（HALO），将物理域升维至超图，利用切比雪夫多项式小波滤波器在谱小波域中进行学习，避免显式超图拉普拉斯特征分解；通过可训练的二元小波尺度实现自适应频响，并引入紧框架正则化保证多尺度覆盖与稳定性。

Result: HALO 在 2D/3D 结构与非结构化网格上均达到最优或近最优性能，支持稳定多步滚动预测；在百万级点云的工业气动几何中表现优于或等同于最强的固定离散化 Transformer，同时保持分辨率等变性。

Conclusion: HALO 为复杂几何下的物理系统建模提供了一种高效、稳定且可扩展的神经算子框架，兼具谱域建模能力与非结构化网格适应性，是当前最先进方法的重要突破。

Abstract: Physical systems are often modeled by solution operators that map input fields, parameters, geometries, or past states to steady or future physical states. Learning these maps is difficult, especially for time-dependent systems that must assimilate history and remain stable under autoregressive rollout. Many neural operators work best on regular, structured grids, while realistic simulations often require unstructured meshes or point clouds to resolve complex geometries; in such settings, grid-centric representations can lose accuracy. Graph neural operators handle these domains through message passing or spectral graph filtering, but pairwise edges do not directly capture group-wise couplings among mesh cells, local neighborhoods, or conservation volumes. We introduce the Hypergraph Adaptive waveLet Operator (HALO), which lifts the domain to a hypergraph and learns in its spectral wavelet domain. HALO avoids explicit hypergraph-Laplacian eigendecomposition through Chebyshev polynomial wavelet filters, giving localized spectral kernels at linear sparse-matrix cost. Its trainable dyadic wavelet scales are regularized toward tight-frame coverage, allowing the frequency response to adapt to each PDE while encouraging stable multi-scale spectral coverage. Across 2D and 3D benchmarks on structured and unstructured discretizations, HALO achieves best or near-best accuracy among frequency-, transformer-, DeepONet-, state-space-, and graph-based baselines and sustains stable multi-step rollouts. The same model scales to industrial aerodynamic geometries: on meshes of a few hundred thousand points it is on par with, or better than, the strongest fixed-discretization transformers, while remaining resolution-equivariant.

</details>


### [254] [There and Back Again: Bidirectional Diffusion Bridges for Multimodality Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27885)
*Gabe Guo,Elon Litman,Thanawat Sornwanee,Jose Blanchet,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BIT提出了一种双向图像-文本扩散桥，通过直接从文本开始并插值到图像，实现了源感知的生成路径和可逆的生成过程，支持灵活的采样算法，并在视觉-语言和自然科学评估中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态翻译方法存在生成路径不直接表示源模态导致采样灵活性受限，且为单向生成无法实现反向（如图像到文本）的问题。

Method: 基于随机微分方程（SDE）推导出双向图像-文本扩散模型，构建了从文本到图像的源感知生成路径和从图像到文本的端点条件过程，支持统一的双向生成框架。

Result: 实验表明，BIT在多个视觉-语言和自然科学研究任务上性能优于去噪扩散和确定性流基线模型，具备良好的扩展性和生成质量。

Conclusion: BIT提供了一个高效、灵活且可逆的多模态生成框架，解决了传统方法在生成路径和方向性上的局限，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Multimodality translation (e.g., text-to-image) is a core generative AI task. However, existing approaches (1) follow generative paths that do not directly represent the source modality, limiting the flexibility of some sampling algorithms; and (2) are unidirectional, preventing inversion (e.g., image-to-text). We propose BIT: Bidirectional Image-Text Diffusion Bridges. In contrast to previous approaches, BIT starts directly from text and interpolates into images, providing (1) a source-aware generative path that enables diverse and flexible sampling algorithms; and (2) an endpoint-conditioned process that can be traversed from image to text, providing a unified, bidirectional generative framework. BIT is derived through stochastic calculus, yielding SDE forms amenable to simulation and tractable loss functions that scale to high dimensions. Our experiments show that BIT is competitive with denoising-diffusion and deterministic-flow baselines, and outperforms them on several vision--language and natural-science evaluations.

</details>


### [255] [TACIT-Switch: Cost-Aware Model Escalation for LLM Agents from Censored Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27911)
*Ji'an Lei,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACIT-SWITCH 是一种学习永久切换策略的方法，利用累积轨迹证据和教师标注的干预时间（TACIT）来决定何时从低成本小模型切换到高成本大模型。该方法将每个标注视为累积风险尺度上的区间删失观测，采用混合治愈阈值模型估计强回放成功的概率及条件成功下的切换阈值，部署时无需教师。在多步模拟中，相较于任务级、步骤级和固定前缀基线，其成功率提升7.4-11.1个百分点且成本相当；消融实验表明任务特征与累积轨迹风险提供互补信息。在开发数据上选择操作点后，TACIT-SWITCH 在 ALFWorld 和 DABench 上均取得最高保留成功率（ALFWorld: 48.5% with 4B Cheap; 45.5% with 9B Cheap；DABench: 73.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型成本低但易陷入持续失败模式，大模型更可靠但代价高，因此需要一种智能路由机制来决定何时切换模型以平衡可靠性与成本。

Method: 提出 TACIT-SWITCH 方法，基于累积轨迹证据和教师标注的干预时间（TACIT），将标注建模为累积风险尺度上的区间删失观测，采用混合治愈阈值模型估计成功概率与切换阈值，训练阶段利用教师信号，部署时无需教师。

Result: 在多步模拟中，成功率比基线提升7.4-11.1个百分点，成本相当；消融实验证明任务特征与轨迹风险信息互补；在 ALFWorld 与 DABench 上分别达到 48.5%、45.5% 和 73.1% 的最高保留成功率。

Conclusion: TACIT-SWITCH 能有效学习可靠的永久切换策略，在不增加部署成本的前提下显著提升复杂任务的成功率，是实现高效可靠智能体的重要进展。

Abstract: Agents with smaller language-model backbones are less expensive but can drift into persistent failure modes, whereas those with larger backbones are generally more reliable but more costly. This reliability-cost trade-off motivates routing methods that decide when to invoke an agent with a larger backbone: before execution, after a fixed trajectory prefix, or locally at individual steps. Our method, TACIT-SWITCH, learns permanent handoff policies from accumulated trajectory evidence and Teacher-Annotated Censored Intervention Times (TACIT). It represents each annotation as an interval-censored observation on a cumulative-risk scale. The resulting mixture-cure threshold model estimates the probability that the paired Strong rollout succeeds and, conditional on success, the handoff threshold; no teacher is required at deployment. In a mechanism-based multi-step simulation, TACIT-SWITCH improves success by 7.4-11.1 percentage points over task-level, step-level, and fixed-prefix routing baselines at comparable cost. Within that controlled simulation, ablations show that task features and cumulative trajectory risk provide complementary information. With operating points selected on development data, TACIT-SWITCH achieves the highest held-out success among learned policies on both ALFWorld (48.5% with 4B Cheap; 45.5% with 9B Cheap) and DABench (73.1%).

</details>


### [256] [TI$^2$PS: A Topology-Informed Inverse Design Framework for Stochastic Multicellular Pattern Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27931)
*Kenji Komiya,Andrew Kailiang Jin,Ryo Nishikimi,Kunio Kashino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新框架，通过结合贝蒂向量和逆代理建模，解决多细胞代理模型（ABM）中细胞级参数估计和拓扑特征量化的问题。该方法在斑马鱼色素模式形成案例中验证有效，仅用10%训练数据即超越使用100%数据的PointNet++，表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多细胞ABM面临两大挑战：一是难以准确估计细胞层面的参数，二是难以对随机增殖与死亡条件下的多细胞空间排列拓扑特征进行定量评估。传统方法在处理复杂模式时存在局限性，亟需更高效、精准的参数推断与模式匹配技术。

Method: 采用拓扑数据分析中的贝蒂向量来表征多细胞空间构型的拓扑特征，并结合逆代理建模实现从目标模式直接反推ABM参数，从而构建可解释性强、泛化能力高的参数估计流程。

Result: 在斑马鱼色素模式形成案例中，该框架成功实现了参数估计，且在所有评估指标上均优于传统方法（如PointNet++），尤其在数据利用率方面表现出显著优势——仅使用10%训练数据即可超越使用100%数据的对比方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为多细胞模式形成的参数估计提供了高效、精确的新路径，具备良好的可扩展性和实际应用潜力，尤其适用于高噪声、高随机性的生物系统建模。

Abstract: This study proposes a novel framework to estimate parameters for reproducing target multicellular patterns using an agent-based model (ABM). Two major challenges in multicellular ABMs are estimating cell-level parameters (agent-specific variables) and quantitatively evaluating the topological characteristics of multicellular arrangements under stochastic cell proliferation and death. To address these challenges, we integrate two approaches: Betti vectors and inverse surrogate modeling. The Betti vectors obtained through topological data analysis can consistently represent features of a wide range of multicellular spatial configurations. The inverse surrogate modeling enables direct inference of the corresponding ABM parameters from the target patterns. We validated the proposed framework using zebrafish pigment pattern formation, a representative model of pattern formation driven by multicellular interactions. The results demonstrate that our framework successfully estimates ABM parameters and outperforms conventional methods such as PointNet++. Notably, the proposed method, which used only 10% of the training data, outperformed PointNet++, which used 100% of the data, across all evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [257] [Temporal Memory-Aware Online Test-Time Adaptation on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27948)
*Bo Li,Xin Zheng,Ming Jin,Can Wang,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DGOTTA框架，用于动态图上的测试时自适应（TTA），通过时间感知增强、记忆感知预测和一致性引导在线适应三个模块，有效应对动态图中结构与语义的持续变化，提升模型在多种分布偏移下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要集中在静态图的测试时自适应，而动态图中的结构和节点语义随时间不断演化，导致传统方法难以适应，因此需要一种新的框架来解决动态图上测试时性能下降的问题。

Method: 提出DGOTTA框架，包含三个模块：(1) 时间感知增强，以扩展测试动态图的多样性；(2) 记忆感知预测，缓解灾难性遗忘；(3) 一致性引导在线适应，确保时间对齐和记忆平滑性。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集和四种DGNN骨干网络上进行的大量实验表明，DGOTTA显著提升了模型在多种分布偏移和不同架构下的泛化性能。

Conclusion: DGOTTA是一种有效的动态图测试时自适应框架，能够有效应对动态图中复杂的时间与空间分布偏移，显著提升模型的可靠性和适应性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) on graphs aims to adapt a graph neural network (GNN) that is well-trained on the training graph to the test graph, which involves potential distribution shifts that may harm model generalization and test-time inference. While recent efforts have investigated TTA on static graphs, there is still a research gap on dynamic graphs learned with dynamic GNN (DGNN) models, where both structural connectivity and node semantics evolve continuously over time. This makes adapting a DGNN model for reliable test-time performance substantially challenging. To fill this gap, in this work, we propose a novel framework of temporal memory-aware Online Test-Time Adaptation on Dynamic Graphs, named DGOTTA, to effectively adapt well-trained DGNNs during test time. Specifically, the proposed DGOTTA contains three modules: (1) temporal-aware augmentation, to extend the diversity of test dynamic graphs for addressing complex temporal and spatial shifts; (2) memory-aware model prediction, to alleviate catastrophic forgetting; (3) consistency-guided online adaptation, to enforce temporal alignment and memory smoothness. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets and four DGNN backbones demonstrate that DGOTTA significantly improves generalization under diverse distribution shifts and multiple model architectures.

</details>


### [258] [PhyMamba: Physics-Modulated Mamba for Robust Battery Health Prognostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27978)
*Sara Sameer,Yunyi Zhao,Wei Zhang,Minggang Zeng,Wenqing Li,Man-Fai Ng,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyMamba，一种两阶段物理调制的Mamba框架，将电化学老化信息融入序列建模，无需依赖侵入式测量即可实现长时序电池健康预测。第一阶段通过轻量级Mamba编码器处理BMS信号并生成潜在表示，经老化参数化模块转化为物理信息特征；第二阶段采用定制Mamba预测主干进行多周期预测，通过物理约束引导模型内部时序更新以符合退化演化规律。在多个公开数据集和预测时长下，PhyMamba显著优于多种基线方法，平均误差降低31.8%，同时具备良好的精度-效率权衡，适合实际部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电池健康预测方法在长时序预测中面临工况依赖性和传感器噪声的挑战，且传统方法常需依赖侵入式测量来识别内部老化参数，限制了其在实际电池管理系统中的应用。

Method: 提出两阶段物理调制的Mamba框架（PhyMamba）：第一阶段使用轻量级Mamba编码器处理BMS信号，结合老化参数化模块生成物理信息特征；第二阶段采用定制Mamba预测主干，通过物理机制紧密集成于时序更新中，实现退化一致的多周期预测。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，PhyMamba在多个预测时长下均取得最优综合性能，相比多种基线方法平均误差降低31.8%，同时展现出优异的精度与效率平衡，支持实际部署。

Conclusion: PhyMamba通过将物理知识嵌入Mamba架构，实现了无需侵入式测量的高精度、长时序电池健康预测，为电池管理系统的实用化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Battery health prognostics is a core function in battery management systems (BMSs), yet long-horizon health forecasting from BMS signals remains challenging due to operating-condition dependency and sensor noise. In this paper, we propose PhyMamba, a two-stage physics-modulated Mamba framework that integrates electrochemical aging into sequence modelling. PhyMamba does not require explicit identification of internal aging parameters, which often relies on intrusive measurements. In stage-1, a lightweight Mamba encoder first processes BMS signals and produces a latent representation that is transformed via an aging parameterization module, into physics-informed aging features. In stage-2, a customized Mamba forecasting backbone performs multi-cycle prediction, where physics is tightly integrated to regulate the model's internal temporal updates toward degradation-consistent evolution. Experiments on three public datasets under multiple forecast horizons show that PhyMamba achieves the best aggregated performance, with an overall mean error reduction of 31.8% compared with a diverse range of baselines. PhyMamba also offers an optimized accuracy-efficiency trade-off, which supports practical deployment for robust battery health prognostics.

</details>


### [259] [Is Monte Carlo Tree Search Just Every-Visit Monte Carlo Control?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27985)
*Xianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）与每访问蒙特卡洛控制在本质上是同一方法的不同表述。尽管前者以搜索术语（选择、扩展、模拟、回溯）描述，后者以强化学习术语（轨迹采样、回报估计、动作值更新、策略改进）描述，但二者在轨迹生成和动作值更新层面并无本质区别。通过将树策略与回滚策略视为单一演进策略的已学与未学部分，扩展对应首次访问初始化，回溯即为标准的每访问蒙特卡洛更新，MCTS的四个阶段可简化为两个基本操作：基于当前策略的轨迹采样与每访问蒙特卡洛更新。因此，MCTS本质上就是以搜索语言和数据结构表达的每访问蒙特卡洛控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清MCTS与每访问蒙特卡洛控制之间的概念混淆，揭示两者在核心机制上的等价性，使研究者更易理解其内在一致性。

Method: 通过对比分析MCTS与每访问蒙特卡洛控制的各个步骤，将它们映射到相同的算法框架下，强调术语差异而非实质不同。

Result: 证明了MCTS与每访问蒙特卡洛控制在轨迹生成和动作值更新层面上具有等价性，仅在表述语言和数据结构上存在差异。

Conclusion: MCTS可以被看作是每访问蒙特卡洛控制的一种以搜索形式表达的方式，二者在算法本质上是统一的。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) and every-visit Monte Carlo (MC) control are usually presented as different methods. MCTS is described in the language of search (selection, expansion, simulation, and backup), whereas MC control is described in the language of reinforcement learning (trajectory sampling, return estimation, action-value updating, and policy improvement). This note argues that, at the level of trajectory generation and action-value updating, the distinction is largely terminological. The tree policy and rollout policy can be viewed as the learned and not-yet-learned parts of a single evolving policy; expansion corresponds to first visit and initialization; and backup is the ordinary every-visit Monte Carlo update. Under this interpretation, the four stages of MCTS reduce to two basic operations: trajectory sampling under the current policy and every-visit Monte Carlo updating. In this sense, MCTS is simply every-visit Monte Carlo control expressed in the language and data structure of search. The purpose of this note is expository: to make this equivalence explicit and easier to recognize.

</details>


### [260] [A Method for Layer Bit-Width Allocation in LLM Quantization via Performance Maximization Under a Quality-Degradation Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28003)
*Artem Safronov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对Gemma-3-1B的分层比特分配方法，旨在在允许的质量损失预算内最大化性能（降低延迟）。与传统耗时的均匀层量化方法（如GPTQ、AWQ）或无性能提升证明的分配方法（如MixLLM、TorchAO）不同，该方法基于先前工作SA-PTQ得到的层敏感性分析，结合TensorRT-LLM中的激活透传模式，对每一层按块独立确定精度（采用5+5、10+10、all26等分组策略），区分FFN、Attention和lm_head对加速的贡献。在RTX 5090上测试了13种W8A8变体，发现对于FFN和lm_head，量化/反量化的时间开销被整数运算带来的加速所抵消；而短上下文长度下，Attention的量化反而增加延迟。为此手动实现了SmoothQuant以适配TensorRT-LLM，因原生导出失败且lm_head不支持。最优方案为FFN 5+5 + lm_head，实现11.0%延迟降低，质量损失极小（Top-1一致率98.90%，困惑度上升0.85%）。若接受更高质量损失，采用FFN all26 + lm_head可实现最高19.1%加速。建议后续优化包括：INT8融合注意力核、KV缓存量化、使用FP8替代INT8，以及类似FFN的局部注意力量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法如GPTQ、AWQ存在时间与资源消耗大问题，而MixLLM、TorchAO等分配策略缺乏明确的性能加速证据。因此需要一种高效、可验证的分层比特分配机制，在控制质量损失的前提下实现显著推理加速。

Method: 基于前期研究SA-PTQ获得的层敏感性分析，采用激活透传模式在TensorRT-LLM中实现分层比特分配；通过5+5、10+10、all26等分组策略，对FFN、Attention和lm_head分别设定不同精度；结合实际硬件（RTX 5090）测量延迟表现，并手动实现SmoothQuant以解决导出兼容性问题。

Result: FFN 5+5 + lm_head配置实现11.0%延迟下降，质量损失极小（Top-1 98.90%，困惑度+0.85%）；若接受更大质量损失，FFN all26 + lm_head可实现最高19.1%加速。

Conclusion: 该分层比特分配方法有效平衡了推理速度与生成质量，在Gemma-3-1B上实现显著加速，且具备可扩展性，未来可通过融合核、KV缓存量化、FP8及部分注意力量化进一步优化性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a layer bit allocation method for Gemma-3-1B, formulating the problem as performance maximization (latency decrease) given a degradation budget constraint (allowable level of generation quality loss). This approach is different from time- and resource-consuming uniform layer quantization methods that are used in the literature (like GPTQ or AWQ) or allocation methods without proven performance-accelerating effect (like MixLLM or TorchAO). The layer sensitivity profile resulting from our prior work SA-PTQ is applied using the activation pass-through mode inside TensorRT-LLM. For each layer precision is determined individually in blocks, according to a grouping introduced in the prior step (5+5, 10+10, all26), differentiating the contribution of FFN, Attention, and lm_head to the overall speedup. The clock speed was measured for 13 W8A8 variants on an RTX 5090. We find that for FFN and lm_head the time cost of quantization/dequantization is compensated for by the use of integer arithmetic, while for short context lengths, the opposite holds true for Attention: an additional step of quantization slows execution down. We propose a manual implementation of SmoothQuant for TensorRT-LLM which was necessary due to export failures, unavailable for lm_head. The best solution found under joint consideration of all three criteria with minimal degradation was FFN 5+5 with lm_head, providing an 11.0% reduction in latency with negligible quality loss (98.90% Top-1 agreement, +0.85% perplexity degradation). With acceptable quality loss for FFN all26 + lm_head, a speedup up to 19.1% was found possible. We suggest further optimizations: fused attention kernels in INT8, KV-cache quantization, using FP8 instead of INT8 and partial Attention quantization analogous to FFN.

</details>


### [261] [Exact Risk Ratios for Weighted Data Selection in Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28007)
*Guangjian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文解决了Hanneke, Moran, Shlimovich和Yehudayoff在COLT 2025中提出的一个开放问题：关于在维度d、样本预算n满足d < n < 2d时，加权最小二乘目标下最小范数ERM的最坏情况误差比F_w(d,n)的精确值。研究证明了对于所有d，F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d；并精确计算了F_w(3,4)=5/3和F_w(4,5)=2。此外，提出了一个基于平衡划分的调和量Γ_{d,k}的下界，并在具有正交电路块结构的白化梯度系统数据集上证明该下界为紧界。所有已知精确值均等于1+Γ_{d,k}，作者据此提出猜想：在整个开区间内等式成立。上界证明依赖于正张成配置的刚性定理、R^3和R^4中小正基的分类与结构简化，以及不依赖维度的极值基论证，将符号锥几何转化为五点选择。论文还提供了反例说明某些简化解法无效，并给出了多项式时间构造算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Hanneke, Moran, Shlimovich和Yehudayoff提出的关于加权最小二乘框架下最小范数经验风险最小化器性能保证的开放问题，特别是填补d < n < 2d这一未解区间的理论空白。

Method: 采用几何分析方法，包括正张成配置的刚性定理、R^3与R^4中小正基的分类与结构简化、维度无关的极值基论证，结合符号锥几何与五点选择机制，构建下界与上界。通过构造特定数据集反例验证非最优路径的失效，并设计多项式时间算法实现有效选择。

Result: 确定了F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d，F_w(3,4)=5/3，F_w(4,5)=2；提出了下界F_w(d,d+k) ≥ 1+Γ_{d,k}，并在具有特定结构的数据集类上证明其紧性；所有精确值均匹配1+Γ_{d,k}，支持全局等式猜想。

Conclusion: 该研究完整刻画了加权最小二乘框架下最小范数ERM在中等样本预算下的最坏情况性能，揭示了其与正基几何结构的深刻联系，并提出统一公式1+Γ_{d,k}作为整个开区间内的精确值猜想，为后续理论研究提供坚实基础。

Abstract: Hanneke, Moran, Shlimovich and Yehudayoff (COLT 2025) posed the following open problem. A selector sees a finite dataset $D \subseteq \mathbb{R}^d \times \mathbb{R}$, picks at most $n$ examples together with nonnegative weights, and hands the weighted least squares objective to the minimum-norm ERM. Writing $F_w(d,n)$ for the worst-case ratio between the loss of the returned predictor on all of $D$ and the optimal loss, they proved $F_w(d,n)=\infty$ for $n<d$, $F_w(d,d)=d+1$ and $F_w(d,n)=1$ for $n \ge 2d$, and asked for the value in the open regime $d<n<2d$. We determine this value in several cases. For every $d$ we prove $F_w(d,2d-1)=1+1/d$, which confirms a claim stated without proof in the original note. We further prove $F_w(3,4)=5/3$ and $F_w(4,5)=2$, the two smallest cells not covered by the endpoint formula. For every intermediate budget $n=d+k$ we prove the lower bound $F_w(d,d+k) \ge 1+Γ_{d,k}$, where $Γ_{d,k}$ is an explicit harmonic quantity over balanced partitions, and we show that this bound is the exact minimax value over the class of datasets whose whitened gradient systems carry an orthogonal circuit-block structure. All three exact values match $1+Γ_{d,k}$, and we conjecture that equality holds throughout the open regime. The upper bound proofs run on a common geometric spine: a rigidity theorem for positive spanning configurations of loss gradients, classifications and structural reductions of small positive bases in $\mathbb{R}^3$ and $\mathbb{R}^4$, and a dimension-free extremal-basis argument that converts sign-cone geometry into five-point selections. We also give explicit counterexamples showing that several shorter routes fail, and constructive polynomial-time selection algorithms for all proved cases.

</details>


### [262] [When Can Conditional Flow Matching Replace Pointwise Negative Log-Likelihood?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28010)
*Yansen Han,Hongxin Sun,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了流匹配（flow matching）中条件流匹配（CFM）损失作为端点负对数似然（NLL）及其差值作为对数似然比的适用性。针对线性高斯路径，作者精确分解了端点NLL，揭示其由熵、加权CFM目标、内部速度-得分残差和边界残差组成。结果表明，仅当这些残差相互抵消时，仅使用CFM的估计和差值才是精确的。在非策略分布最优情况下，普通CFM并非一般意义上的点态NLL估计器；而权重 $w_{\mathrm{sc}}(t)=(1-t)/t$ 可消除内部残差，但此性质不普遍适用于训练或策略内对齐。即使端点分布相同或经过代理优化，策略内对数比仍可能有偏。实验在不同维度、分布和几何结构下验证了结论及不精确比率仍具实用性的机制。更广泛地，该分解为将基于似然的LLM方法适配到流匹配提供了理论基础，并区分了精确替换与受控代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在流匹配框架中准确使用条件流匹配（CFM）损失作为负对数似然（NLL）及其差值作为对数似然比，以解决现有方法中潜在的偏差问题。

Method: 通过线性高斯路径的数学分析，精确分解端点负对数似然（NLL），引入熵、加权CFM目标、内部速度-得分残差和边界残差等组成部分，系统评估CFM估计与实际NLL之间的偏差来源。

Result: 普通CFM在非策略最优下不能作为点态NLL估计；特定权重 $w_{\mathrm{sc}}(t)=(1-t)/t$ 可消除内部残差，但该性质不普遍成立；策略内对数比仍可能有偏，即使端点分布一致或经代理优化。实验支持理论发现，且揭示不精确比率在实践中仍具价值。

Conclusion: 该研究为将基于似然的方法适配至流匹配提供了理论依据，明确区分了精确替换与受控代理，强调了对残差影响的深入理解对于提升模型可靠性的重要性。

Abstract: Flow matching enables likelihood-free training, yet alignment methods increasingly reuse conditional flow matching (CFM) losses as endpoint negative log-likelihoods (NLLs) and their old/new differences as log-likelihood ratios. We characterize when these substitutions are valid. For linear Gaussian paths, we exactly decompose endpoint NLL into entropy, a weighted CFM objective, an interior velocity--score residual, and a boundary residual. Thus CFM-only estimates and differences are exact only when the corresponding residuals cancel. At the off-policy population optimum, ordinary CFM is not generally a pointwise NLL estimator, whereas \(w_{\mathrm{sc}}(t)=(1-t)/t\) removes the interior residual; this positive result does not extend generally to training or on-policy alignment. On-policy log-ratios can remain biased even for identical endpoint laws or after surrogate optimization. Experiments across dimensions, distributions, and geometries support these conclusions and the mechanisms that make inexact ratios useful. **More broadly, the decomposition provides a theoretical basis for adapting likelihood-based LLM methods to flow matching, while distinguishing exact substitutions from controlled surrogates.**

</details>


### [263] [Explainable Uncertainty Estimation for Reliable Medical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28052)
*Li Rong Wang,Jamie Duell,Xinran Xu,Thomas C. Henderson,Yu Yue Hew,Pik Wan Erica Chiang,Xiao Wei Alstar Ang,Bingwen Eugene Fan,Xiuyi Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为egRUE的可解释不确定性估计方法，将不确定性估计与可解释人工智能（XAI）相结合，不仅量化预测的不确定性，还能提供特征层面的解释。通过理论证明和实验验证，该方法在可靠性与可解释性上优于现有方法，并在医学专家用户研究中显示其能提升对正确预测的信心并降低对错误预测的信任，从而增强医疗决策支持系统的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人工智能在医疗领域的应用受限于医生对其预测结果的信任不足。虽然不确定性估计和可解释人工智能（XAI）各自有助于提升信任，但它们通常被分开处理，缺乏对不确定性来源的特征级解释，无法指导如何通过补充检测来减少不确定性。

Method: 提出一种名为预期梯度重构不确定性估计（egRUE）的新方法，将预测解释融入不确定性计算过程，能够分解不确定性到各个特征层面，实现对不确定性成因的可解释分析。

Result: 实验表明，egRUE在预测可靠性与可解释性方面均优于现有方法；医学专家用户研究表明，结合特征级解释后，医生对模型输出的信任更加合理——对正确预测更有信心，对错误预测则更警惕。

Conclusion: 通过融合不确定性估计与特征级解释，egRUE显著增强了人工智能在高风险医疗场景中的决策支持能力，既指明了预测可能不可靠的情况，也揭示了导致不确定性的关键因素，为临床信任提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has strong potential to support clinical decision-making, yet its adoption in healthcare remains limited due to a lack of trust. Uncertainty estimation can signal unreliable predictions, and explainable AI (XAI) can clarify how predictions are made but existing methods treat them separately, providing no feature-level insight into why a prediction is uncertain or which tests to prioritize to reduce it. To address this gap, we propose explainable uncertainty estimation, which unifies uncertainty estimation and XAI to both quantify uncertainty and explain feature-level contributions. We introduce the Expected Gradients Reconstruction Uncertainty Estimate (egRUE), which incorporates prediction explanations into its uncertainty computation and decomposes uncertainty into feature-wise contributions. We prove theoretical properties of egRUE and show through experiments that it improves reliability and interpretability compared to existing methods. A user study with medical experts further demonstrates that egRUE's explanations improve calibrated trust over uncertainty scores alone, increasing confidence in correct predictions and reducing confidence in incorrect ones. By combining prediction uncertainty with feature-level explanations, egRUE strengthens decision-making support in safety-critical healthcare settings, clarifying both when predictions may be unreliable and which features drive that uncertainty.

</details>


### [264] [Comparing Classical and Quantum Machine Learning for Regression in High Energy Physics Collision Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28084)
*Tariq Mahmood,Zain ul Abidin,Itzel Luviano Soto,Alfredo Raya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了经典机器学习模型（SVM、ANN、CNN、LSTM）与量子版本（QSVM、QNN、QCNN、QLSTM）在高能物理粒子碰撞事件分类与回归任务中的表现。基于CERN开放数据集，使用横向动量分量作为输入特征，横向动量大小作为回归目标。结果显示，在当前硬件和数据集条件下，经典模型（尤其是CNN和LSTM）性能略优；但量子模型在参数效率上表现突出，例如QCNN仅用4个量子比特和深度为3的电路即可达到深度经典CNN的性能，显示出近中期量子设备上的参数优势。该工作为未来实际量子硬件上的研究提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实验高能物理中，粒子碰撞事件的分类与回归是计算挑战，需处理大量模拟数据且要求速度与精度并重。随着量子机器学习的发展，探索其在该领域的潜力成为重要方向。本研究旨在评估经典与量子模型在真实资源受限条件下的性能权衡，为后续研究提供基准。

Method: 对四种经典机器学习架构（SVM、ANN、CNN、LSTM）及其对应的量子版本（QSVM、QNN、QCNN、QLSTM）进行系统性对比。使用来自CERN Open Data Portal的质子-质子碰撞模拟数据，以横向动量分量为输入特征，横向动量大小为回归目标。所有模型在相同数据集和训练条件下进行训练与评估，并分析其性能与参数数量的关系。

Result: 经典模型（特别是CNN和LSTM）在定量性能上略优于量子模型，但在参数效率方面，量子模型表现出显著优势。例如，QCNN仅用4个量子比特和深度为3的电路即达到深度经典CNN的性能，表明其在近中期量子设备上具有真正的参数效率优势。此外，基线分析确认该回归问题对浅层多项式拟合非平凡，支持了架构比较的必要性。

Conclusion: 在当前硬件和数据限制下，经典模型仍具微弱性能优势，但量子模型展现出显著的参数效率，尤其适合资源受限的近中期量子设备。该研究为未来在真实量子硬件上开展相关研究提供了关键基准和指导。

Abstract: The classification and regression of particle collision events constitute a persistent computational challenge in experimental high energy physics, where large volumes of simulated data must be processed with both speed and precision. This work carries out a systematic comparison of four classical machine learning architectures, support vector machines (SVM), artificial neural networks (ANN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and long short-term memory (LSTM) networks against their quantum counterparts: quantum SVM (QSVM), quantum neural networks (QNN), quantum CNN (QCNN), and quantum LSTM (QLSTM). All models are trained on simulated proton-proton collision events with electron-positron and muon-antimuon final states from the CERN Open Data portal, using transverse-momentum components as input features and transverse-momentum magnitude as the regression target. Classical architectures, and in particular the CNN and LSTM, achieve marginally better quantitative performance under current hardware and dataset constraints. Quantum models, however, reach competitive accuracy with substantially fewer trainable parameters: the QCNN reproduces the performance of the deep classical CNN using only four qubits and a circuit of depth three, pointing to a genuine parameter-efficiency advantage on near-term quantum devices. A baseline analysis confirms that the regression problem is non-trivial for shallow polynomial fits, supporting the relevance of the architectural comparison. These results characterize the trade-offs between classical and quantum approaches under realistic, resource-constrained conditions and provide a benchmark for future studies on actual quantum hardware.

</details>


### [265] [Generalized Gibbs Ensemble Weighting for Forecast Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28116)
*Prasen R. Nuthanakaluva,Nava K. Gaddam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GGEW is a probabilistic framework for adaptive forecast combination using Gibbs-style weighting, with variants like Stable Gibbs, Directional Gibbs-NCL, and Symmetric Gibbs-NCL. It employs numerical stabilization, diversity-aware corrections, and online hyperparameter adaptation via a UCB-style mechanism (Local-UCB). Evaluated on M4 competition data and real-world datasets, GGEW shows strong and consistent performance across diverse settings, though its effectiveness varies by dataset and conditions. The work presents a flexible framework rather than a universally superior method.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing simple aggregation rules for forecast combination (e.g., mean, median, inverse-loss weighting) perform well but lack adaptability to varying conditions such as dataset characteristics, forecast horizons, and disagreement levels among models. There is a need for a more robust, adaptive framework that can dynamically adjust weights based on predictive performance and model diversity.

Method: GGEW uses a Gibbs-style exponential weighting rule based on normalized predictive loss. It introduces numerical stabilization, diversity-aware score corrections, and online hyperparameter tuning via the Local-UCB bandit algorithm to adapt learning rate, diversity strength, and variant selection in real time without full grid search.

Result: GGEW and its variants outperform or match standard baselines across multiple benchmark datasets (M4, Monash Traffic Hourly, Electricity Hourly, Solar Weekly) under different deployment protocols and forecast horizons. Performance varies by context, indicating that adaptive Gibbs weighting is effective but not universally dominant.

Conclusion: GGEW provides a flexible, adaptive framework for forecast combination that leverages dynamic weighting based on loss and diversity. While not universally superior, it demonstrates strong empirical performance and opens avenues for further research into when and why adaptive weighting strategies are beneficial.

Abstract: Forecast combination is a reliable way to improve predictive performance when several forecasting models are available. Simple aggregation rules such as the mean, median, trimmed mean, inverse-loss weighting, and exponential weighting are often strong baselines, but their relative performance can vary across datasets, forecast horizons, deployment settings, and levels of disagreement among base forecasters. We develop Generalized Gibbs Ensemble Weighting (GGEW), a probabilistic framework that treats forecasting models as experts and assigns ensemble weights using a Gibbs-style exponential transformation of normalized predictive loss. The framework extends this basic weighting rule through numerical stabilization, diversity-aware score corrections, and online hyperparameter adaptation. GGEW produces a family of related methods, including Stable Gibbs weighting, Directional Gibbs-NCL, and Symmetric Gibbs-NCL. These variants share one core algorithm and differ only in the score used inside the exponential weighting rule. For sequential deployment, we adopt a UCB-style bandit mechanism, called online Local-UCB, to adapt the learning rate, diversity strength, and Gibbs variant without evaluating the full hyperparameter grid at every prediction step. We evaluate GGEW on official M4 competition forecast submissions and external rolling-origin deployment experiments using Monash Traffic Hourly, Electricity Hourly, and Solar Weekly datasets. Results suggest that Gibbs-style adaptive weighting is a useful and competitive tool across several benchmark settings, although its relative performance varies across datasets, forecast horizons, deployment protocols, and forecast disagreement groups. The contribution is not a universal dominance claim, but a framework and empirical study motivating further investigation of when adaptive Gibbs-style forecast combination is useful.

</details>


### [266] [VICT: Verifier-Instrumented Credit Tracing for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28128)
*Pengcheng Li,Zhengyang Zhang,Dongxu Zhang,Sui Huang,Shaohua Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VICT提出一种新的信用分配方法，通过暴露验证器内部的可执行原子和证据，将信用沿着依赖有效证明边回溯到动作，从而在训练时实现细粒度信用分配。该方法不改变原始奖励，仅在训练时调整优势张量，无需学习的批评者、过程标签、分支回滚或推理时的验证器访问，在ALFWorld和WebShop上表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习中的信用分配在长周期大语言模型代理中面临挑战，现有方法虽尝试从回滚侧获取更细粒度信用，但忽略了验证器内部的任务结构信息，导致信息损失。

Method: 提出VICT（VerifierInstrumented Credit Tracing），利用验证器内部的可执行原子与证据，构建依赖有效证明边，仅沿这些边重新分配组相对优势，实现从回滚侧推断到验证器侧追踪的转变。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop任务中，VICT显著优于仅基于结果的训练方法，并达到与最新细粒度信用方法相当的性能；消融实验排除了密集原子奖励、最终提交信用、时间接近性及稀疏性作为充分解释因素。

Conclusion: VICT通过利用验证器内部结构实现高效且精确的信用分配，无需额外学习组件或复杂机制，是一种轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-grained credit assignment is a central challenge in reinforcement learning for long horizon LLM agents. Standard objectives often train from programmatically verifiable terminal rewards by broadcasting each sparse outcome to every action in a trajectory. Existing methods typically seek finer credit from the rollout side, constructing auxiliary trajectory signals or additional comparisons to estimate action importance. Although useful, these approaches still treat the verifier that judged success as a scalar reward, discarding its internal task structure. Our key insight is that many verifiable tasks already encode the relevant checks inside their terminal verifier. We propose VICT (VerifierInstrumented Credit Tracing), a training-time interface that exposes executable or evidence backed atoms and traces them back to actions through dependency-valid proof edges. VICT redistributes group-relative advantage only along those edges, shifting credit assignment from rollout-side inference to verifierside tracing. It preserves the original terminal reward, abstains when evidence is incomplete or ambiguous, and changes only the training-time advantage tensor, requiring no learned critic, process labels, branch rollouts, or inference-time verifier access. On ALFWorld and WebShop, VICT improves substantially over outcome-only training and achieves strong performance alongside recent fine-grained credit methods; ablations rule out dense atom rewards, final-commit credit, temporal proximity, and sparsity as sufficient explanations.

</details>


### [267] [Learning to Difference: Adaptive Reversible Differencing (AdaRDiff) for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28134)
*Morad Laglil,Younes Hlal,Marouane El Hadari,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaRDiff 是一种可学习的自适应可逆差分方法，通过加权差分简化时间序列，去除趋势和季节性后进行预测，并通过自回归重建恢复原始结构。该方法支持并行化计算，显著提升速度（最高33.7倍），在8个基准数据集上达到最先进的预测精度，且参数开销极低，可作为即插即用模块提升多种模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统差分方法依赖人工设定的阶数和周期，难以融入现代深度学习架构；而时间序列中的趋势与季节性结构复杂，影响预测性能，亟需一种自动、高效且可集成的差分机制。

Method: 提出 AdaRDiff，使用可学习权重对历史时刻进行加权差分，生成稳定残差用于预测；预测后通过闭式卷积表达式自回归重建原始成分，实现趋势与季节性的联合建模，并支持GPU并行加速。采用两阶段训练策略，分离结构发现与重建学习，以优化梯度传播。

Result: 在8个不同领域（电力、天气、交通、能源）的基准测试中，AdaRDiff均达到或超过现有方法的最优性能；相比线性模型提升高达25.9%，相比iTransformer提升18.3%；计算效率提升最高达33.7倍；参数量几乎无增加。

Conclusion: AdaRDiff 提供了一种高效、通用且可扩展的时间序列差分框架，能够有效建模复杂时间结构，在保证高精度的同时极大提升计算效率，适合作为多种模型的即插即用组件。

Abstract: Reliable long-horizon time series forecasting is an important yet difficult problem. Trends and seasonality introduce complex temporal structure that challenges learning-based forecasting models. Differencing, which subtracts nearby past values to remove such structure, is the classical remedy, but its reliance on hand-picked orders and periods has kept it largely absent from recent deep architectures. We propose \textbf{\underline{Ada}}ptive \textbf{\underline{R}}eversible \textbf{\underline{Diff}}erencing \textbf{(AdaRDiff)}, a generalized differencing approach that uses learnable weights to simplify the series through weighted differencing with previous time instants. This yields stabilized residuals on which forecasting is performed, after which the removed components are restored autoregressively to reconstruct the forecast, capturing trend and seasonality jointly through a single operator. This reconstruction admits a closed-form convolutional expression, which parallelizes on GPU and yields up to $33.7\times$ speedup over the naive recurrence. We furthermore rely on a two-phase training schedule that separates temporal structure discovery from reconstruction learning, as suggested by a theoretical analysis of the gradient when using a linear forecasting model. AdaRDiff attains state-of-the-art forecast accuracy across eight benchmarks spanning electricity, weather, traffic, and energy, at negligible parameter cost. Furthermore, it is designed as a plug-and-play module: integrating AdaRDiff improves eight diverse backbones, from linear models to Transformers, in the large majority of cases, by up to $25.9\%$ with a linear backbone and $18.3\%$ with iTransformer.

</details>


### [268] [Conditional Diffusion Models for Energy-Efficient Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28142)
*Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,André Snoeck,Chyi-Fu Hong,Shijing Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种条件扩散框架，用于生成基于路线特征（如车速、环境温度）的电动汽车电池电流轨迹。该模型结合潜在条件编码器与时间1D U-Net去噪主干，将行程相关条件映射到共享表示并引导逆扩散过程。在包含12,000次行程的公开数据集上评估，生成的电流轨迹真实捕捉了主要时序包络和瞬态事件，其Wasserstein距离仅为0.0029，显著低于真实数据间的参考距离0.0085。相比直接条件注入，所提方法使Wasserstein距离降低89.1%，MAE降低52.8%。该框架为真实运行条件下电动汽车能耗建模提供了生成式解决方案，支持不确定性感知的大规模车队规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型多提供确定性点估计或有限不确定性摘要，无法充分反映实际运营中所需的可变能量消耗轨迹，限制了电动货运车队的优化决策。因此需要更精确、能捕捉多样性和不确定性的能源消耗建模方法。

Method: 提出一种潜变量条件扩散模型，采用潜变量编码器对路线特征进行编码，并通过时间1D U-Net作为去噪主干，在反向扩散过程中利用条件信息生成高保真电池电流轨迹。

Result: 模型在真实数据集上生成的电流轨迹具有高度真实性，能准确捕捉主导时序模式和尖锐瞬态事件；其生成分布与实测分布的Wasserstein距离为0.0029，远低于真实数据间距离0.0085；相较于直接条件注入，性能显著提升，Wasserstein距离下降89.1%，MAE下降52.8%。

Conclusion: 本研究构建了一种适用于真实工况下电动汽车能耗建模的生成式框架，能够有效表征能量消耗的不确定性，为大规模车队运营中的不确定性感知规划提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Electrification of commercial delivery fleets is shifting fleet routing from distance- and time-based optimization toward energy-aware decision-making. Existing sequence models primarily provide deterministic point estimates or limited uncertainty summaries, which do not capture the range of plausible energy-consumption trajectories required for operational decision-making. In this work, we introduce a conditional diffusion framework that generates EV battery-current profiles conditioned on route features such as vehicle velocity and ambient temperature. The model combines a latent conditioning encoder with a temporal 1D U-Net denoising backbone that enables trip-related conditions to be mapped into a shared representation and guides the reverse diffusion process. We evaluate the framework on an open-access commercial EV telemetry dataset containing 12k trips from 9 vehicles. The proposed latent-conditioned diffusion model generates realistic cur- rent trajectories that capture both the dominant temporal envelope and sharp transient events. The model achieves a Wasserstein distance of 0.0029 between generated and measured current distributions below the real vs real reference distance of 0.0085 indicating that generated samples lie within the empirical variability of the test set. We further demonstrate that learned latent conditioning substantially improves performance over direct condition injection, reducing the Wasserstein distance by 89.1% and MAE by 52.8%. This work demonstrates a generative modeling framework for characterizing EV energy consumption under real-world operating conditions, providing an essential foundation for uncertainty-aware fleet planning in large-scale operational settings.

</details>


### [269] [The Approximation Rank of Softmax Attention: Sharp Geometric Laws and Robust Interaction Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28150)
*Yuhe Sui,Jianing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了归一化softmax注意力的秩复杂度由何种几何结构决定，发现支持几何结构决定了最坏情况下的温度缩放，而softmax可见的交互几何结构控制每个头的近似复杂度。研究提出了两种尖锐的最坏情况定律：球面自注意力的秩为Θ_{d,ε}(min{n,(1+β)^{(d-1)/2}})，而全球几何结构在满足一定条件下可达到Θ_{d,ε}(β^{d/2})。对于固定头，行softmax消除了行标量逻辑方向，剩余的查询-键交互维度r带来r/2的每实例上界，且构造证明该指数是极小极大最优的。近似交互子空间引入显式残差输出误差，并产生一个容差索引的SVD维数。在BERT-base校准数据集上的实验显示，在多种头-温度设置下存在适度的有效维数降低，并与有限构造秩上界证书呈正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究归一化softmax注意力的秩复杂度受何种几何结构控制，以理解其内在复杂性机制，并区分支持几何与可见交互几何对模型复杂度的影响。

Method: 通过分析最大行ℓ₁逼近秩，建立基于支持几何的数学模型，推导出不同几何结构（球面、全球）下的秩复杂度界限；利用行softmax特性分离标量方向，分析剩余交互维度；通过构造性方法验证上界紧致性，并在真实模型（BERT-base）上进行实验验证。

Result: 球面自注意力的秩为Θ_{d,ε}(min{n,(1+β)^{(d-1)/2}})，全球几何结构在特定条件下可达Θ_{d,ε}(β^{d/2})；交互维度r对应每实例秩上界为r/2，且该指数为极小极大最优；实验表明有效维数降低并正相关于构造秩上界。

Conclusion: 支持几何决定了最坏情况下的温度缩放行为，而softmax可见的交互几何结构则主导单个头的近似复杂度，二者在理论上和实证上均被分离，揭示了注意力机制复杂性的双重几何根源。

Abstract: Which geometry controls the rank complexity of normalized softmax attention? We study maximum-row-$\ell_1$ approximation rank, exactly the least unrestricted rank preserving every bounded vector-valued output. Two sharp worst-case laws isolate support geometry: for fixed $d$ and error $\varepsilon$, spherical self-attention has rank $Θ_{d,\varepsilon}(\min\{n,(1+β)^{(d-1)/2}\})$, while full-ball geometry adds one radial degree and, for $β\geβ_0(d,\varepsilon)$ and $n\ge C_d e^{β/8}$, gives $Θ_{d,\varepsilon}(β^{d/2})$. For a fixed head, row-softmax quotients out row-scalar logit directions: the remaining visible query--key interaction dimension $r$ yields an $r/2$ per-instance upper law, and bounded constructions show this exponent is minimax sharp. Approximate interaction subspaces incur an explicit residual output error and yield a tolerance-indexed SVD dimension. On an 84-head BERT-base calibration set, we observe modest effective-dimension reductions across many head--temperature settings, together with positive associations with finite constructive rank upper certificates. Together, these results separate support geometry, which sets worst-case temperature scaling, from softmax-visible interaction geometry, which controls per-head approximation complexity.

</details>


### [270] [HARTS: Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Hybrid-Attention Models over Arbitrary Rollout Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28158)
*Boyuan Meng,Peihua Bao,Hong Liu,Xiaowei Zhu,Chao Wang,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.LG
